### Q005  DISCO ELYSIUM (the whole-game quest MODEL + The Tribunal)
- `Q005.P1` **W1 (dialogue is gameplay** validates our pacifist-completable spine; keep proving conflicts resolve socially/morally without combat (our Q038/Q047/Q049). Combat is ONE verb, never the only one.
- `Q005.P10` **AVOID (the brick wall, C1** never gate a Bohemia quest CHAIN behind one un-passable check; always a reroute (ask a companion to step in, a different approach, a costlier path).
- `Q005.P2` **W2 (internal party / skills-as-voices** we don't need 24, but bank a LIGHT "instinct voices" system — the dynasty's hard-won TRAITS (from upbringing Q003 + prior quests) speak up in dialogue as competing nudges (the ruth
- `Q005.P3` **W3 (failure-as-content** THE priority upgrade for our skill-gates. Write the FAILURE branch of key [READ]/[BARTER]/social checks as its own real content (fail the read -> accuse the wrong person -> a new p
- `Q005.P4` **W4 (red vs white** formalize TWO check types in Bohemia — retryable (grind/return) vs ONE-SHOT permanent (the succession vote, the endgame threshold, a life-or-death faction beat). Flag the permanent
- `Q005.P5` **W5 (identity-as-loot** our unrecorded ledger + succession ALREADY make identity the reward; push it — some "rewards" (a hardening trait, a fearsome reputation) should carry a DOWNSIDE as characterization
- `Q005.P6` **W6 (dress-for-the-check** let players tilt Bohemia odds via visible, swappable means (gear, a companion present, a faction favor spent) — transparent soft modifiers, not dice-reloading.
- `Q005.P7` **W7 (the unavoidable collision** our Q039 summit + Q050 threshold are inevitable collisions; keep building climaxes that HAPPEN regardless, with agency in the HOW (who survives, on what terms).
- `Q005.P8` **W8 (odds = the investigation** make our climaxes silently read the whole prior playthrough (Q050 already reads the Reconstruction; extend: how you're "dressed," who you brought, what you learned).
- `Q005.P9` **W10 (moralized failure** tie failure to our THEME — a dynasty's failures are the subject (consequence across time), not a punishment; the fold surfaces them as story (Q042), never as a game-over slap.
- `Q005.W1` **DIALOGUE IS THE GAMEPLAY** no combat; every conflict is conversation + checks. Proof a great quest needs zero fighting — the tension is social, moral, internal.
- `Q005.W10` **MORALIZED FAILURE, NOT PUNISHED** failing is embedded in the game's THEME (a failure of a man in a failed world) — failure isn't a slap, it's the subject. ===========================================================
- `Q005.W2` **THE INTERNAL PARTY (skills as voices)** externalize the character's competing impulses as VOICES that argue — the player's own psyche is the "party," and the build changes what you perceive.
- `Q005.W3` **FAILURE-AS-CONTENT** write the FAILURE branch as richer than success wherever possible; teach the player (by making failure fun) that reloading is the worse experience. Failure = new story.
- `Q005.W4` **RED vs WHITE (permanent vs retryable)** distinguish one-shot story-altering checks from retryable ones; concentrate the biggest divergences in clearly-flagged permanent moments (the Tribunal).
- `Q005.W5` **IDENTITY-AS-LOOT (Thought Cabinet)** reward engagement with CHARACTER (ideas, self-concept), not gear — and let some rewards make you WORSE, as characterization. The build is a self-portrait.
- `Q005.W6` **DRESS-FOR-THE-CHECK (transparent soft modifiers)** let players tilt odds via visible, swappable means (clothing/Thoughts) — agency over probability without save-scumming.
- `Q005.W7` **THE UNAVOIDABLE COLLISION** some climaxes should be INEVITABLE (the Tribunal happens no matter what); agency lives in HOW you meet it, not whether. Fixed event + variable survival.
- `Q005.W8` **THE ODDS ARE THE INVESTIGATION** everything you did earlier (who you talked to, what you learned, how you built/dressed) silently stacks the climax's checks — the payoff of paying attention.
- `Q005.W9` **PERCEPTION IS BUILD-GATED** high skills reveal lines/clues invisible to other builds — replay value + the sense of a world bigger than any one playthrough sees.

- `Q006.W9` **BEATABLE BY UNDERSTANDING, NOT GRINDING** you can save everyone with smart assignment even under- prepared, or lose people while "completing" everything — mastery is comprehension, not checklist.

- `Q018.W4` **"THERE IS ALWAYS A WAY" (the trust contract)** every unreachable thing HAS a knowledge-key; the world never cheats. That reliability is what makes players keep exploring intrinsically.
- `Q018.W5` **KNOWLEDGE-GATES DISGUISED AS SKILL-GATES** the hard zones yield to UNDERSTANDING (drift past the blind fish), not reflexes/gear — "I figured it out" beats "I leveled up."
- `Q018.W6` **THE CLOCKWORK WORLD** the solar system changes through the loop; be at the right place at the right TIME — a self-directed living-clock (cf. Anju/Kafei, Questbook 11; ME2 clock, Questbook 06).
- `Q018.W7` **THE LOOP AS A UNIT OF LEARNING** die/reset keeps only memory + log — failure costs nothing but time, and every loop banks knowledge. Death is a teacher, not a punishment (cf. Disco failure-as-content, Questbook 05
- `Q018.W8` **THE PRESSURE IS GENEROUS** the 22-min clock creates urgency without tyranny (generous fuel/oxygen) — a soft deadline that focuses without frustrating (the Anju-easing lesson, Questbook 11).
- `Q018.W9` **THE EMOTIONAL PAYOFF OF COMPREHENSION** the finale lands because you UNDERSTAND — knowledge resolves into an emotional/philosophical acceptance (cf. Planescape's regret, Questbook 12; our Liberate).

### Q019  THE DEDUCTION ENGINE (Return of the Obra Dinn)
- `Q019.P1` **Our [READ] investigations + the Amalgamation (built from data-PORTRAITS of the dead) + the unrecorded ledger make Obra Dinn's forensics unusually on-theme for us** 
- `Q019.P2` **W1/W2 (deduction is gameplay + inverted mystery** build a Bohemia DEDUCTION quest where the dynasty identifies the dead — PERFECT for the Amalgamation, literally an archive of uploaded DATA-PORTRAITS: a quest to match faces->names
- `Q019.P3` **W3/W6 (holistic read + logic-mesh** make our investigations reward reading the WHOLE scene/ledger and cross-referencing across quests — a clue to one person found in another's record; the unrecorded ledger as a logic
- `Q019.P4` **W4 (the witness-the-instant verb** a Bohemia "Memento Mortem" equivalent — the Amalgamation's stored memories let the dynasty WITNESS a frozen recorded moment (a death, an upload, a founder's choice) and walk it for
- `Q019.P5` **W5 (death-chaining** unlock our mystery in deducible ORDER by chaining — witnessing one recorded moment reveals the next reachable one (a structure for the Amalgamation dive, Q050 approach).
- `Q019.P6` **W7 (the RULE OF THREE — the big steal** adopt a VALIDATION mechanic for our deduction/social content — lock in conclusions in sets (or on cross-confirmation) so the player can't blind-guess, but a well-reasoned inference
- `Q019.P7` **W8/W9 (no hard fail + easing ramp** let our hardest deduction/mystery content be PRESSURE-FREE (solve at leisure) and EASE near the end — respect the player's pace (validated across Outer Wilds/Anju).
- `Q019.P8` **W10 (fully fair by design — the CONTRACT** if we build deduction, EVERY answer must be fairly clueable — no ambiguous "gotcha" (regression-gate our mysteries for solvability; Obra Dinn's few under-clued fates are the cautio
- `Q019.W1` **DEDUCTION IS THE GAMEPLAY** observation + cross-reference + inference ARE the mechanics — no combat, no upgrades; the PLAYER's reasoning is the engine (cf. Outer Wilds knowledge-progression, Q18).
- `Q019.W10` **FULLY FAIR BY DESIGN (the contract)** enough info to solve everything without guessing — AND the honest flaw: where the art/clue is ambiguous, the contract breaks. Fairness is non-negotiable for deduction games. ======
- `Q019.W2` **THE INVERTED MYSTERY** face -> method -> name (you don't know who anyone IS) — flipping the detective structure creates a novel, deeper puzzle. Start from maximum ignorance.
- `Q019.W3` **THE HOLISTIC SCENE-READ** the clue is rarely on the corpse — read the WHOLE tableau (who's near whom, ranks, clothing, absence) — every scene is a resource for identifying OTHERS. Look at everyone.
- `Q019.W4` **THE WITNESS-THE-INSTANT VERB (Memento Mortem)** a single evocative verb (freeze + walk the moment of death) that's both narrative and mechanical — the mechanic IS the horror and the clue-source.
- `Q019.W5` **DEATH-CHAINING (memory within a memory)** unreachable subjects become reachable by chaining from scenes where they appear — an elegant unlock structure that reveals the story in deducible order.
- `Q019.W6` **THE LOGIC-MESH (lateral information)** most solutions need facts from OTHER scenes — the puzzle interlocks, so accrued knowledge cascades (solving one unlocks three). Cross-reference is the joy.
- `Q019.W7` **THE RULE OF THREE (validation without hand-holding)** lock fates in sets of 3 — prevents blind brute-force AND lets a reasoned guess ride on two certainties. The system that makes a hard mystery FAIR and solvable. (The single most por
- `Q019.W8` **NO HARD FAIL, NO TIMER** deduce at your leisure; "storm coming" just signals completeness — pressure- free thinking (cf. Outer Wilds' generous clock, Q18). Respect the player's pace.
- `Q019.W9` **THE DIFFICULTY EASES AT THE END** 3-correct drops to 2-correct near the finish — a hidden ramp that rewards progress and softens the tail (cf. Anju N64->3DS easing, Q11).
- `Q019.X1` **A few fates are genuinely UNDER-CLUED / ambiguous** the monochrome, pixelated art can make a death unreadable (is the beast biting, eating, poisoning?), and some fates lean on very obscure observation. LESSON: if deduction is the ga
- `Q019.X2` **BRUTE-FORCE IS STILL POSSIBLE on low-option fates (when only 2 choices remain) — the Rule of Three mitigates but doesn't fully prevent guessing** LESSON: validation systems reduce but rarely eliminate cheese; design clue-density so guessing is unrewarding. >> LAW: validation systems reduce but rarely eliminate cheese; design

### Q021  "YOU CAN'T SAVE EVERYONE" (Pathologic 2)
- `Q021.P1` **Bohemia is a HARDCORE post-collapse survival RPG with death-math + scarcity currencies (medicine, electricity, resources) — Pathologic 2 is the master text on making SCAR** (We are NOT trying to be as punishing; steal the PRINCIPLES + the tuning honesty.)

- `Q021.P3` **W3 (slow-onset cruelty** stage a Bohemia crisis so the pressure CREEPS (calm early, dire by mid) — the player learns the stakes by living them (ties our generational pacing; a dynasty's decline felt, not a
- `Q021.P4` **W4 (the body/city as a second clock** our survival meters + life-support buildings ARE this — mere survival costs resources + time, forcing moral compromise under scarcity (validate; scarcity should manufacture hard ba
- `Q021.P5` **W5 (triage as the core loop** a Bohemia TRIAGE quest — allocate scarce medicine/power/food across districts/people/factions with never enough; every save is a loss elsewhere; the allocation IS the ethics (ties 
- `Q021.P6` **W6 (odds not guarantees** our treatment/rescue outcomes should be PROBABILITIES the dynasty influences, not guarantees — live with results you shaped but didn't control (ties ME2 math Q06, our combat dial o
- `Q021.P7` **W7 (the diegetic questlog** consider making a Bohemia "log" diegetic — the unrecorded ledger / a dynast's mindmap AS the quest tracker (story informs the interface; cf. Obra Dinn Q19, Outer Wilds rumor-web Q1
- `Q021.P8` **W8 (no-clean finale, genuinely debated** our Liberate/Respect/Become already are this — two-plus reasonable end-choices players flip on, where "winning" is questionable — validated by the master of bleak moral finales.
- `Q021.P9` **W10 (suffering as design + THE TUNING FIX** THE crucial honest port — if we lean into scarcity-grief, ship DIFFICULTY SLIDERS (our Difficulty Packages already exist for combat!) so hardcore players get the full cruelty and o
- `Q021.W1` **TIME AS A RESOURCE TO LOSE (not beat)** the clock isn't a challenge to master — it's a scarcity to grieve; missing events/deaths is BUILT IN. The most distinctive time design in the medium.
- `Q021.W10` **SUFFERING AS DESIGN (+ the tuning-knob fix)** the costs make the choices heavy — but it's alienating, so difficulty sliders let players set their own cruelty. Powerful + honest about its own hard sell. ========================
- `Q021.W2` **"YOU CAN'T SAVE EVERYONE" AS THE THESIS** inevitable loss is the CORE experience, not a fail state — the game mechanizes triage and grief; you play to manage failure, not avoid it.
- `Q021.W3` **THE SLOW-ONSET CRUELTY** the pressure is absent early and creeps in by day 4-5 — you LEARN the cruelty by living it, so it lands as lived experience, not a rules-explanation.
- `Q021.W4` **THE BODY AS A SECOND CLOCK** hunger/thirst/exhaustion/infection make mere survival cost time + resources — scarcity forces moral compromise (theft, hard bargains) organically.
- `Q021.W5` **TRIAGE AS THE CORE LOOP** prioritize districts/people/factions with never enough — every save is a death elsewhere; the gameplay IS the ethics (cf. That Lucky Old Sun allocation, collection).
- `Q021.W6` **ODDS, NOT GUARANTEES** treatment raises survival PROBABILITY, never certainty — you live with outcomes you influenced but didn't control (cf. ME2 survival math Q06). Uncertainty = weight.
- `Q021.W7` **THE DIEGETIC QUESTLOG (mindmap)** the quest log IS the character's mind — story informs the very interface; tracking the plot is inhabiting the protagonist (cf. Obra Dinn Q19, Disco Q05).
- `Q021.W8` **THE NO-CLEAN FINALE (town vs magic)** two reasonable, well-argued end-choices players genuinely flip on — and by the end "winning" is itself questionable (so few survive). Debate as the payoff.
- `Q021.W9` **THE METANARRATIVE FAIL-STATE** miss the ending and the game breaks the fourth wall to berate you — form commenting on failure; the game is ABOUT its own unforgivingness.
- `Q021.X1` **DELIBERATELY MISERABLE / NOT FOR EVERYONE** the survival meters can dominate to the game's "detriment" (you spend most of your time managing your body, not the story); the suffering is the design, and it's a hard sell. Ice-P
- `Q021.X2` **UN-100%-ABLE BY DESIGN** you can't see everything in one run; some love it, many find it a hard sell. LESSON: missable content deepens replay + theme but frustrates completionists — a real trade-off to mak

- `Q022.W10` **THE THEME IS A QUESTION, NOT A LECTURE** "what is it to be human?" is asked through situations, never preached — the player arrives at the despair/hope themselves (cf. Planescape Q12, Forgotten City Q20). ================

### Q023  THE NO-MARKER QUESTLINE (Elden Ring / FromSoftware)
- `Q023.P1` **Our MAP LAW (Claude never designs layouts) means SPATIAL cryptic-ness is Paolo's call — but the STORYTELLING techniques + the hard-won cautions are ours to bank** Our single-DISTRICT scale is actually the ANTIDOTE to Elden Ring's core flaw (cryptic works better in enfolded spaces).
- `Q023.P2` **W1/W2 (world-as-quest-log + the stumble-thrill** our single district is SMALL enough that marker-less discovery can work WITHOUT the scale problem — bank OPTIONAL diegetic questlines found by listening/ observing (a rumor, an ove
- `Q023.P3` **W3 (item/object descriptions as lore** bury Bohemia lore in OBJECT flavor (a salvaged photo, a pre-collapse receipt, a data-portrait's caption) the curious assemble — the Amalgamation is LITERALLY built of data-fragment
- `Q023.P4` **W4 (environmental storytelling** our tile/prop pipeline can narrate wordlessly (a barricaded home, a shrine of old currency, a child's toy in the ruins) — the district testifies (validates our art philosophy; the 
- `Q023.P5` **W5 (interlocking NPCs** weave early "side" Bohemia NPCs into later major threads (the shopkeeper you bartered with in gen 1 is central to a gen-2 crisis) — one tapestry (ties our fold + DOS2/KCD).
- `Q023.P6` **W6 (NPCs progress independently** our scheduler can let NPCs live/move/leave/die on world-time whether witnessed or not — presence has stakes (ties Pathologic Q21 missable events, NV pre-seeding).
- `Q023.P7` **W7 (a side quest that is an ending** our Liberate/Respect/Become already tie deep questlines to ENDINGS — validated; keep making the biggest optional threads pay off in worldview + finale.
- `Q023.P8` **W9 (main gates side + vice versa** interlock our main Reconstruction spine with side quests structurally (a main milestone unlocks a side location; a side bond gates a main option) — not just narrative links (ties M
- `Q023.P9` **CAUTIONS (bank ALL of them** our single-district scale mitigates the missable-at-scale flaw, but STILL (a) make EVERY quest fairly clueable in-world (Obra Dinn/Elden-Ring fairness contract); (b) TELEGRAPH orde
- `Q023.W1` **THE WORLD IS THE QUEST LOG** no markers/log — quests live in dialogue, item text, and the environment; the PLAYER assembles them. Discovery is earned and personal (the anti-checklist).
- `Q023.W10` **THE FALSE BINARY REJECTED (the lesson from the CRITICISM)** "reveal nothing vs reveal everything" is a false choice — the FIX is better in-world clues/dialogue/notes, NOT markers-or-nothing. Diegetic signposting is the craft. ==============
- `Q023.W2` **THE QUIET THRILL OF STUMBLING ON THE NEXT STEP** finding progression yourself (a re-visited NPC, a read description) is a discovery a waypoint would destroy — intrinsic-motivation gold.
- `Q023.W3` **ITEM DESCRIPTIONS AS LORE** bury the deep story in flavor text the curious ASSEMBLE — narrative as an archaeological dig (cf. Obra Dinn Q19, Vault 22 Q03).
- `Q023.W4` **ENVIRONMENTAL STORYTELLING** architecture/corpses/placement narrate history wordlessly — the space testifies (cf. the Bloody Baron's house Q01, Vault 22 Q03).
- `Q023.W5` **INTERLOCKING NPCs (early side = later thread)** a character met on an unrelated early errand (Blaidd) turns out central to a major questline — quests WEAVE into one tapestry (cf. DOS2 Q15, KCD Q08).
- `Q023.W6` **NPCs PROGRESS INDEPENDENTLY** characters live/move/die on world-time whether you watch or not — an alive, indifferent world (cf. NV pre-seeding, ME2/Anju clocks). Presence has stakes.
- `Q023.W7` **A SIDE QUEST THAT IS AN ENDING** Ranni's questline yields a whole game ENDING + worldview — the ultimate "side content is the game" (cf. ME2 loyalty Q06, BG3 Nightsong Q16).
- `Q023.W8` **THE TRAGEDY SIGNATURE** NPC quests mostly end sadly — a consistent melancholy that unifies the world's tone (cf. NieR Q22, Witcher Q01). Tonal coherence through recurring fate.
- `Q023.W9` **MAIN GATES SIDE (and vice versa)** a MAIN boss (Radahn) gates a SIDE quest's key location (Nokron) — the main and side content interlock structurally, not just narratively.
- `Q023.X1` **MISSABLE-BY-DESIGN AT SCALE** without markers, on a huge map, players lose whole questlines or must wiki them — the immersion the design protects gets broken by the wiki it necessitates. The core tension. LESSO
- `Q023.X2` **HIDDEN ORDERING DEPENDENCIES** doing NPC-B's quest can silently break NPC-A's, with no warning — feels like "unsatisfying nonsense" to players who trip it. LESSON: if order matters, TELEGRAPH it, or make failure
- `Q023.X3` **INCONSISTENT QUALITY** Ranni's is praised (solvable if you listen); others (Nepheli's) are cited as poorly-clued even by fans of the style. LESSON: the approach only works if EVERY quest is fairly clueab
- `Q023.X4` **THE "BAND-AID" PATCH** adding optional markers later pleased new players but, critics argue, removed the thrill WITHOUT fixing scale. LESSON: bolted-on waypoints are a blunt fix; design signposting DIEGE

- `Q024.P1` **Our unrecorded ledger + standing + fold are a reactivity engine; Alpha Protocol is the master cult text on making it a WEB with per-person, multidimensional, mechanics-re** 
- `Q024.P2` **W1/W8 (web not binary + delayed seeds** our ledger/fold already track a web; bank Alpha Protocol's depth target — per-FACTION and per-PERSON consequence, much of it paying off a generation later (our Q040->042). Not good
- `Q024.P3` **W2 (intent over script, timed** consider a Bohemia timed INTENT choice for high-pressure beats (a standoff, a negotiation, a betrayal) — pick a STANCE under a clock, commit to a persona (pairs with the Deus Ex so
- `Q024.P4` **W3/W4 (no universal-correct + like vs respect** make Bohemia standing MULTIDIMENSIONAL — a faction/ NPC can FEAR, RESPECT, TRUST, or RESENT the dynasty, each with different aid, and the persona that wins the Reds may lose the Ch
- `Q024.P5` **W5/W6 (reputation buys mechanics + boss-altering** THE standout port — let Bohemia relationships/intel re-tune COMBAT + city-systems: an ally poisons/sabotages an enemy before a fight; a faction favor cuts the power to a Network st
- `Q024.P6` **W7 (intel as social power / dossiers** our [READ] + the ledger ARE dossiers — collecting intel on an NPC/faction should UNLOCK dialogue/leverage (a bluff you can't make without the file) — knowing is power (ties Obra Di
- `Q024.P7` **W9 (prep shapes options** our city-builder economy IS the prep layer — what you built/stockpiled/allied before a crisis shapes your options in it (ties ME2 wind-up Q06, our Reconstruction).
- `Q024.P8` **W10 + FLAWS (surface the consequence + BOTH layers must work** CRUCIAL cautions — (a) our combat + city-builder must be FUN on their own (Alpha Protocol's reactivity couldn't rescue weak gameplay; our Dead-Eye dial + build loop must stand alon
- `Q024.W1` **REACTIVITY AS A WEB, NOT A BINARY** track relationships + consequences across factions/people, not a good/evil bar — 32 endings from a web of small choices. The game REMEMBERS everything.
- `Q024.W10` **SURFACE THE CONSEQUENCE (the FLAW-fix)** deep reactivity risks being unseen — ACKNOWLEDGE what the player did (an NPC references it, an ending tallies it) so it lands even on one playthrough. =============================
- `Q024.W2` **INTENT OVER SCRIPT (timed stances)** pick an ATTITUDE under a clock, not exact words — models real-conversation pressure + commits you to a persona (cf. Deus Ex social battle Q17, Mass Effect wheel).
- `Q024.W3` **NO UNIVERSALLY-CORRECT CHOICE** the stance that wins one NPC loses another — reputation is per-person and taste-based; you can't please everyone (cf. NV factions Q02, but per-individual).
- `Q024.W4` **LIKE vs RESPECT (multidimensional reputation)** NPCs can respect you MORE for defying them — fear/ admiration/trust/resentment are distinct axes with distinct payoffs. Antagonism can be a valid, rewarded persona.
- `Q024.W5` **REPUTATION BUYS MECHANICS (incl** combat): relationships re-tune the game — an ally poisons a boss's drugs; intel skips a mission. Consequence reaches into moment-to-moment MECHANICS, not just story.
- `Q024.W6` **BOSS-ALTERING CONSEQUENCE** the deepest reactivity changes a BOSS FIGHT based on a past conversation — the narrative and mechanical layers fuse (a conversation three missions ago makes this fight easier).
- `Q024.W7` **INTEL AS SOCIAL POWER (dossiers)** collecting info on an NPC UNLOCKS dialogue/leverage — knowing them is manipulating them (cf. CASIE Q17, Lady Boyle deduction Q14).
- `Q024.W8` **DELAYED, INVISIBLE-SEED CONSEQUENCE** many choices only pay off hours later — the world has a long memory (cf. Whispering Hillock Q04, ME2 Q06, BG3 Q16).
- `Q024.W9` **PREP SHAPES OPTIONS** vendor/intel prep before missions determines what's possible — preparation as a reactivity input (cf. ME2 wind-up Q06).
- `Q024.X1` **REACTIVITY TOP-TIER, MOMENT-TO-MOMENT WEAK** the near-universal verdict — the choice/consequence model is legendary, but the shooting/stealth gameplay "isn't fun and doesn't really work." The game is carried ENTIRELY by its r
- `Q024.X2` **REACTIVITY IS INVISIBLE / UNREWARDED ON ONE PLAYTHROUGH** much of the brilliance (e.g. maxed CQC breaks Brayko's unblockable combo; 32 endings) is only visible across MULTIPLE playthroughs — "you'd never know unless you played it that way
- `Q024.X3` **SHORT GAME, GROWS ON REPLAY** rated ~7/10 first run, "+1 per playthrough" — built for replay, a hard sell for one-and-done players (cf. Pathologic Q21, Elden Ring Q23 missable content).

- `Q041.X3` **UNRELENTING TONE** the gothic despair is relentless; some bounce off the constant grimness. LESSON: even a dread-game needs texture + the occasional triumph (the Virtue high, a clutch win) to stay be
- `Q041.X4` **STRESS-HEAL DOMINANCE** optimal play centers on stress-healers/prevention, which can narrow team comps. LESSON: don't let one counter-system become mandatory — keep varied answers to attrition viable. >>

### Q051  YOU START AS NOBODY / THE WORLD THAT DOESN'T REVOLVE AROUND YOU (Gothic)

- `Q128.W3` **THE MIDPOINT IS A WOMAN DECLINING RESCUE** The pendant delivered, Anju chooses to stay in a doomed town for a maybe. The player enables the bravest decision in the game and is not consulted on it.
- `Q128.W4` **SHAME IS DEFEATED BY PUNCTUALITY** Kafei's arc has no persuasion node — he is not talked out of hiding; he keeps an appointment as he is. Arrival is the apology.
- `Q128.W5` **THE WEDDING ASKS YOU TO LEAVE** The quest's final beat excludes its player: the Couple's Mask is handed over and the door is closed. Witness, then absence — the reward includes being dismissed from it.
- `Q128.W6` **FAILURE IS AMBIENT** Miss an hour and no screen tells you; the world just quietly holds its sadder configuration. The chain fails the way real plans fail: without ceremony.
- `Q128.W7` **THE PRIZE SURVIVES THE ERASURE AND THE MARRIAGE DOESN'T** Rewind to save the world and only the mask and the player remember. The quest gives the player a memory with no witnesses — carrying it IS the reward's final form.

- `Q128.W9` **HOPE TRAVELS BY MAIL** The chain's crucial links are LETTERS with collection times — intimacy routed through infrastructure. The corpus's best marriage of logistics and longing (cf. Q101's supply-chain r
- `Q128.X1` **THE GUIDE PROBLEM** Three days of exact hours across a dozen locations: the community verdict for 25 years is that blind completion is near-mythical. Theme-in-optional's terminal form: THEME-IN-SCHEDU
- `Q128.X10` **THREE-DAY REPETITION FATIGUE** Every retry replays known beats in real time; the quest's respect for schedules extends to punishing yours.

- `Q128.X6` **KAFEI'S CURSE IS A PLOT COUPON** Who cursed him and why (Skull Kid, offhand) is set dressing; the quest's engine never touches its own inciting magic.

- `Q128.X8` **RESEMBLANCE-TRUST IS UNEXAMINED** Anju trusts the mask's face; the quest never prices what it means that hope will follow a costume. Bohemia MUST price this (the Amalgamation is made of resemblances).

- `Q129.X7` **TENPENNY HIMSELF ESCAPES THE THEME** The owner's indifference is never priced by any branch; the building's god outlives every configuration of its tenants (absent an unrelated sniper option elsewhere).
- `Q129.X8` **THE EVICTED VANISH** Residents pushed out for the deal exit the simulation; the diplomacy route's own displaced have no downstream existence.
- `Q129.X9` **MORAL AFTERSHOCK HAS NO MECHANICAL HOME** The player's only processing tools are a gun and a fast-travel map; companions don't comment; the world doesn't reference it. The loudest silence in the game.

- `Q130.P3` **TRUTH BILLED TO THE TELLER [W3]** The Fold tracks a per-NPC CREDIBILITY line: honest corrections of a motivated believer SPEND it. Bohemia makes the real-world economics of truth-telling mechanical — and never edit
- `Q130.P4` **THE MAINTENANCE BRANCH, NAMED [W5, F3 fixed]** The stall-state gets one discovery hook (a townsperson: "he's had 'investigators' before, you know") so the darkest branch is findable. The Fold logs maintenance income under its o
- `Q130.P5` **THE WATCHED WORLD WATCHES BACK [F8 fixed]** Bohemia's tails have counter-surveillance: targets notice on a schedule; being seen following writes ITS own consequences. Surveillance becomes a two-ledger game — direct feed for 
- `Q130.P6` **THE AMALGAMATION AS GLARTHIR AT SCALE [the lore port]** The NETWORK's threat-model is a paranoid client with infrastructure: it pays (in access, in power) for CONFIRMATIONS of threats to itself, and the dynast's reports write to a machi
- `Q130.W1` **THE CLIENT'S REALITY IS PLAYER-WRITABLE** Reports are writes to a man's world-model, and the model has a body count. No quest in the corpus makes the pen this literal.
- `Q130.W10` **NOBODY HAS TO DIE AND SOMEONE USUALLY DOES** Every corpse in every branch is optional and authored; the quest's violence is 100% player-written through an intermediary who believes you. Responsibility with one layer of launde
- `Q130.W2` **THE GAMEPLAY TEACHES THE TRUTH BEFORE THE FORK ASKS** You WATCH the innocence for three days before any node lets you deny it — the tails are the evidence; the reports are the confession.
- `Q130.W3` **TRUTH COSTS THE TRUTH-TELLER** Honest reports spend your standing in his ledger, not his theory's. The corpus's cleanest model of why people stop correcting the paranoid: correction is expensive and billed to th
- `Q130.W4` **THE LIE IS FRICTIONLESS AT POINT OF SALE** Confirmation costs one dialogue click and pays gold; the invoice arrives days later with names on it. Fear-feeding priced exactly like real life: cheap now, catastrophic on deliver
- `Q130.W5` **MAINTENANCE IS A BRANCH** The stall — managing a sick man forever for coin, curing nothing, killing no one — is the quest's darkest mirror and it's just... available, unmarked, indefinitely.
- `Q130.W6` **THE HYPOTHESIS HAS NO NODE** "What if you're wrong" is absent because HE has no slot for it; the option list is a psychograph. Interface as the client's mind.
- `Q130.W7` **THE HELP-VERB FIRES BLANKS** "You need a healer" is true, kind, and useless — the quest lets decency be correct and ineffective at once, which almost nothing in the medium permits.
- `Q130.W8` **THE INSTITUTION'S MERCY HAS INSTITUTIONAL GRANULARITY** The guard exit works and is exactly as gentle as a city watch gets: the honest price of outsourcing a sick man to public order.
- `Q130.W9` **THE MIDNIGHT STAGING IS THE DIAGNOSIS** Chapel shadows, passed notes, dawn drops — the quest wraps you in the paranoid's dramaturgy until following his stagecraft feels like procedure. Form as contagion.
- `Q130.X1` **THE TARGETS ARE CERTIFIED INNOCENT** The game never permits the tails to find ANYTHING ambiguous; the player's knowledge is total, which makes the lie-branch pure villainy instead of judgment under uncertainty. LAW: t
- `Q130.X10` **AFTERMATH IS A ZERO** Whoever dies, Skingrad resumes; no funeral, no gossip-state, no guard-log entry readable later. The town has no memory organ (the anti-Fold).
- `Q130.X2` **GLARTHIR'S ILLNESS IS A MONSTER COSTUME** The quest uses paranoia as a plot engine and exits through an axe; no branch models care beyond the guard's blade. Period-typical, banked as such, not excused.
- `Q130.X3` **THE STALL BRANCH IS INVISIBLE** Indefinite management — the most interesting exit — is undiscoverable except by accident; no rumor, no node names it.
- `Q130.X4` **THE KARMA WAKES INCONSISTENTLY (see node note): sleeping through brokered massacres elsewhere, alert to these retail ones** The meter's selective hearing, exhibit N.
- `Q130.X5` **THE TAILS ARE MECHANICALLY THIN** Following schedules in Oblivion is walking slowly behind a pathing NPC; the surveillance fantasy outruns its verbs.
- `Q130.X6` **TRUTH'S COST IS UNDER-TELEGRAPHED** That honest reports move suspicion onto YOU is discoverable only by suffering it; one in-world hint exists (townsfolk warnings), thin.
- `Q130.X7` **THE GUARD EXIT IS TOO CHEAP** One sentence to Dion closes the whole machine at any point; the institutional door has no toll (cf. Q126 F7's four-bypass gate).
- `Q130.X8` **NO NEIGHBOR EVER NOTICES THE TAIL** Three days of being followed by an armored stranger registers with no one; the watched world doesn't watch back.
- `Q130.X9` **THE GOLD CURVE REWARDS FEEDING THE FEAR** Payment scales with confirmations; the economy editorializes toward the lie (reward-gradient law, Q121's corollary, violated here in the WRONG direction on purpose or not — undecid

- `Q134.W3` **THE GOD IS PATIENT FROM INSIDE THE ROOM** No timer, no escalation, no punishment for refusal — just a door that stays a wall. Pressure as pure subtraction: everything removed except compliance.

- `Q134.W6` **PRIDE COMPLETES THE LURE** Tell Logrolf the truth and he comes anyway, to defy. The quest lets honesty and outcome fully decouple — clean mouth, identical hands.

- `Q134.W8` **THE REWARD ARRIVES MID-SWING** The mace restores in your grip during the beating — payment materializing while the arm still moves. Complicity's receipt printed in real time.

- `Q134.X2` **THE STALL HAS NO CONTENT** The refusal loop — the design's best idea — is empty: no escalating dialogue, no house-state changes, no Bal commentary on your stubbornness. Patience UNWRITTEN is just a wall. LAW
- `Q134.X3` **TYRANUS'S TURN IS INSTANT** The Vigilant's mind goes in seconds; the quest spends its most interesting casualty as a tutorial fight. His slow version is the better quest.

- `Q134.X7` **THE VIGILANTS NEVER RESPOND** An order dedicated to exactly this loses a man to exactly this and the faction has no reaction state. The world's immune system, absent again (Q133 F8's twin).
